````rust
//! Slicing with Bambu Studio: shared job state fed by `slicer://job`.

pub mod bridge;
pub mod state;
pub mod types;

use leptos::prelude::*;
use wasm_bindgen_futures::spawn_local;

use types::JobView;

/// Every job the app knows, by ascending id. `App` provides it once, so
/// the Slice page and the STL indicator see the same jobs and events that
/// arrive while the page is closed aren't lost.
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub struct SlicerShared {
    pub jobs: RwSignal<Vec<JobView>>,
}

impl Default for SlicerShared {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            jobs: RwSignal::new(Vec::new()),
        }
    }
}

impl SlicerShared {
    /// One job, as a memo so a row only re-renders when that job changes.
    pub fn job(self, id: u64) -> Memo<Option<JobView>> {
        let jobs = self.jobs;
        Memo::new(move |_| jobs.with(|js| js.iter().find(|j| j.id == id).cloned()))
    }
}

/// Registers the `slicer://job` listener and loads the jobs the backend
/// already has. The listener can't be removed, so this is mounted exactly
/// once, outside anything that can unmount.
#[component]
pub fn SlicerEvents() -> impl IntoView {
    let SlicerShared { jobs } = expect_context::<SlicerShared>();
    crate::agent::bridge::listen::<JobView>("slicer://job", move |view| {
        jobs.update(|js| state::upsert(js, view));
    });
    spawn_local(async move {
        if let Ok(list) = bridge::jobs().await {
            jobs.try_update(|js| {
                for v in list {
                    state::upsert(js, v);
                }
            });
        }
    });
}
````

Create `src/pages/slice.rs`:

````rust
//! Slice page: slice a model with Bambu Studio before printing, and compare
//! filaments side by side. BambuMate never prints; "Open in Bambu Studio"
//! hands the sliced file to the user.

use leptos::prelude::*;
use leptos_router::hooks::use_query_map;
use wasm_bindgen::JsCast;
use wasm_bindgen_futures::spawn_local;

use crate::commands::{self, StlFile};
use crate::slicer::state::{
    compare_rows, format_cost, format_duration, format_grams, status_line, totals,
};
use crate::slicer::types::{
    JobState, PresetLists, PresetOption, PresetSource, SlicerSettings, SlicerStatus, WarningLevel,
};
use crate::slicer::{bridge, SlicerShared};

/// Up to three filaments next to the main one: four columns at most.
const MAX_EXTRA: usize = 3;
/// Jobs listed under "Recent".
const RECENT: usize = 10;

/// Reads a dropped file and stages it in the backend, which needs a path.
async fn stage_dropped(file: web_sys::File) -> Result<String, String> {
    use js_sys::{ArrayBuffer, Uint8Array};
    let buf: ArrayBuffer = wasm_bindgen_futures::JsFuture::from(file.array_buffer())
        .await
        .map_err(|_| "Couldn't read that file.".to_string())?
        .dyn_into()
        .map_err(|_| "Couldn't read that file.".to_string())?;
    let bytes = Uint8Array::new(&buf).to_vec();
    bridge::stage_model(
        file.name(),
        crate::pages::print_analysis::base64_encode(&bytes),
    )
    .await
}

fn file_name(path: &str) -> String {
    path.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(path).to_string()
}

/// A preset `<select>`; user presets first, under their own group.
#[component]
fn PresetSelect(
    id: &'static str,
    label: &'static str,
    options: Signal<Vec<PresetOption>>,
    value: RwSignal<String>,
) -> impl IntoView {
    let group = move |source: PresetSource| {
        options
            .get()
            .into_iter()
            .filter(move |o| o.source == source)
            .map(|o| {
                let selected = o.name == value.get_untracked();
                let label = o.name.clone();
                view! { <option value=o.name selected=selected>{label}</option> }
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    };
    view! {
        <label class="sl-field" for=id>
            <span class="nd-label">{label}</span>
            <select
                id=id
                prop:value=move || value.get()
                on:change=move |ev| value.set(event_target_value(&ev))
            >
                <optgroup label="Your presets">{move || group(PresetSource::User)}</optgroup>
                <optgroup label="Bambu presets">{move || group(PresetSource::System)}</optgroup>
            </select>
        </label>
    }
}

#[component]
pub fn SlicePage() -> impl IntoView {
    let shared = expect_context::<SlicerShared>();
    let status = RwSignal::new(None::<SlicerStatus>);
    let lists = RwSignal::new(PresetLists::default());
    let bed_types = RwSignal::new(Vec::<String>::new());
    let saved = RwSignal::new(SlicerSettings::default());
    let printer = RwSignal::new(String::new());
    let process = RwSignal::new(String::new());
    let filament = RwSignal::new(String::new());
    let bed_type = RwSignal::new(String::new());
    let model = RwSignal::new(None::<String>);
    let watched = RwSignal::new(Vec::<StlFile>::new());
    let comparing = RwSignal::new(false);
    let extra = RwSignal::new(Vec::<String>::new());
    let compare_ids = RwSignal::new(Vec::<u64>::new());
    let selected = RwSignal::new(None::<u64>);
    let error = RwSignal::new(None::<String>);
    let busy = RwSignal::new(false);
    let drag_over = RwSignal::new(false);
    // Only the newest presets request may write its answer.
    let preset_gen = StoredValue::new(0u64);

    // `/slice?job=ID` opens that job (the STL indicator links here).
    let query = use_query_map();
    Effect::new(move |_| {
        if let Some(id) = query.with(|q| q.get("job")).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()) {
            selected.set(Some(id));
            comparing.set(false);
        }
    });

    let load_presets = move |for_printer: Option<String>| {
        let gen = preset_gen.get_value() + 1;
        preset_gen.set_value(gen);
        spawn_local(async move {
            let Ok(l) = bridge::presets(for_printer).await else {
                return;
            };
            if preset_gen.try_get_value() != Some(gen) {
                return;
            }
            let fix = |sig: RwSignal<String>, opts: &[PresetOption]| {
                let current = sig.try_get_untracked().unwrap_or_default();
                if !opts.iter().any(|o| o.name == current) {
                    sig.try_set(opts.first().map(|o| o.name.clone()).unwrap_or_default());
                }
            };
            fix(process, &l.processes);
            fix(filament, &l.filaments);
            if printer.try_get_untracked().unwrap_or_default().is_empty() {
                printer.try_set(
                    l.printers
                        .first()
                        .map(|o| o.name.clone())
                        .unwrap_or_default(),
                );
            }
            lists.try_set(l);
        });
    };

    spawn_local(async move {
        if let Ok(st) = bridge::status().await {
            status.try_set(Some(st));
        }
        match bridge::get_settings().await {
            Ok(view) => {
                let eff = view.effective;
                saved.try_set(view.saved);
                bed_types.try_set(view.bed_types);
                process.try_set(eff.process.unwrap_or_default());
                filament.try_set(eff.filament.unwrap_or_default());
                bed_type.try_set(eff.bed_type.unwrap_or_default());
                printer.try_set(eff.printer.unwrap_or_default());
            }
            Err(e) => {
                error.try_set(Some(e));
            }
        }
        if printer.try_get_untracked().unwrap_or_default().is_empty() {
            load_presets(None);
        }
        if let Ok(files) = commands::list_received_stls().await {
            watched.try_set(files);
        }
    });

    // Processes and filaments follow the printer.
    Effect::new(move |_| {
        let p = printer.get();
        if !p.is_empty() {
            load_presets(Some(p));
        }
    });

    let processes = Signal::derive(move || lists.with(|l| l.processes.clone()));
    let filaments = Signal::derive(move || lists.with(|l| l.filaments.clone()));
    let printers = Signal::derive(move || lists.with(|l| l.printers.clone()));
    let can_slice = move || {
        model.with(|m| m.is_some())
            && !printer.with(String::is_empty)
            && !process.with(String::is_empty)
            && !filament.with(String::is_empty)
            && !busy.get()
            && status.with(|s| s.as_ref().is_some_and(|s| s.supported))
    };

    let browse = move |_| {
        spawn_local(async move {
            match bridge::pick_model().await {
                Ok(Some(path)) => {
                    model.try_set(Some(path));
                }
                Ok(None) => {}
                Err(e) => {
                    error.try_set(Some(e));
                }
            }
        });
    };

    let on_drop = move |ev: web_sys::DragEvent| {
        ev.prevent_default();
        drag_over.set(false);
        let Some(file) = ev
            .data_transfer()
            .and_then(|dt| dt.files())
            .and_then(|f| f.get(0))
        else {
            return;
        };
        busy.set(true);
        error.set(None);
        spawn_local(async move {
            match stage_dropped(file).await {
                Ok(path) => {
                    model.try_set(Some(path));
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    error.try_set(Some(e));
                }
            }
            busy.try_set(false);
        });
    };

    let slice = move |_| {
        let Some(path) = model.get_untracked() else {
            return;
        };
        let (p, q, f, b) = (
            printer.get_untracked(),
            process.get_untracked(),
            filament.get_untracked(),
            bed_type.get_untracked(),
        );
        let mut all = vec![f.clone()];
        if comparing.get_untracked() {
            all.extend(extra.get_untracked().into_iter().filter(|e| !e.is_empty()));
        }
        let remember = SlicerSettings {
            printer: Some(p.clone()),
            process: Some(q.clone()),
            filament: Some(f),
            bed_type: Some(b.clone()),
            auto_slice: saved.get_untracked().auto_slice,
        };
        busy.set(true);
        error.set(None);
        spawn_local(async move {
            let mut ids = Vec::new();
            for fil in all {
                match bridge::slice(path.clone(), p.clone(), q.clone(), fil, Some(b.clone())).await
                {
                    Ok(job) => {
                        ids.push(job.id);
                        shared
                            .jobs
                            .try_update(|js| crate::slicer::state::upsert(js, job));
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        error.try_set(Some(e));
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            if let Some(first) = ids.first() {
                selected.try_set(Some(*first));
            }
            compare_ids.try_set(if ids.len() > 1 { ids } else { Vec::new() });
            if let Ok(view) = bridge::set_settings(remember).await {
                saved.try_set(view.saved);
            }
            busy.try_set(false);
        });
    };

    let toggle_compare = move |_| {
        let on = !comparing.get_untracked();
        comparing.set(on);
        if on && extra.with_untracked(Vec::is_empty) {
            extra.set(vec![String::new()]);
        }
    };

    let recent_ids = move || {
        shared.jobs.with(|js| {
            js.iter()
                .rev()
                .take(RECENT)
                .map(|j| j.id)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        })
    };

    view! {
        <div class="page slice-page nd">
            <header class="sl-head">
                <h2>"Slice"</h2>
                <p class="nd-label sl-version">
                    {move || match status.get() {
                        Some(s) if s.supported => format!("Bambu Studio {}", s.version.unwrap_or_default()),
                        Some(_) => "Bambu Studio unavailable".to_string(),
                        None => "Checking Bambu Studio…".to_string(),
                    }}
                </p>
            </header>

            {move || status.get().and_then(|s| s.message).map(|m| view! {
                <p class="sl-error sl-status-error">{m}</p>
            })}

            <section class="sl-input">
                <div
                    class="sl-drop"
                    class:sl-drop-over=move || drag_over.get()
                    on:dragover=move |ev: web_sys::DragEvent| {
                        ev.prevent_default();
                        drag_over.set(true);
                    }
                    on:dragleave=move |_| drag_over.set(false)
                    on:drop=on_drop
                >
                    <p class="sl-model">
                        {move || match model.get() {
                            Some(p) => file_name(&p),
                            None => "Drop an STL or 3MF here".to_string(),
                        }}
                    </p>
                    <div class="sl-drop-actions">
                        <button class="sl-btn sl-browse" on:click=browse>"Choose file"</button>
                        <Show when=move || !watched.with(Vec::is_empty)>
                            <select
                                class="sl-watch"
                                on:change=move |ev| {
                                    let v = event_target_value(&ev);
                                    if !v.is_empty() {
                                        model.set(Some(v));
                                    }
                                }
                            >
                                <option value="">"From watch folder"</option>
                                {move || watched.get().into_iter().map(|f| view! {
                                    <option value=f.path.clone()>{f.filename.clone()}</option>
                                }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                            </select>
                        </Show>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="sl-presets">
                    <PresetSelect id="sl-printer" label="Printer" options=printers value=printer />
                    <PresetSelect id="sl-process" label="Process" options=processes value=process />
                    <PresetSelect id="sl-filament" label="Filament" options=filaments value=filament />
                    <label class="sl-field" for="sl-bed">
                        <span class="nd-label">"Plate"</span>
                        <select
                            id="sl-bed"
                            prop:value=move || bed_type.get()
                            on:change=move |ev| bed_type.set(event_target_value(&ev))
                        >
                            {move || bed_types.get().into_iter().map(|b| view! {
                                <option value=b.clone()>{b.clone()}</option>
                            }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                        </select>
                    </label>
                </div>

                <Show when=move || comparing.get()>
                    <div class="sl-compare-pickers">
                        {move || (0..extra.with(Vec::len)).map(|i| {
                            let value = move || extra.with(|e| e.get(i).cloned().unwrap_or_default());
                            view! {
                                <label class="sl-field">
                                    <span class="nd-label">{format!("Compare with {}", i + 2)}</span>
                                    <select
                                        class="sl-compare-filament"
                                        prop:value=value
                                        on:change=move |ev| {
                                            let v = event_target_value(&ev);
                                            extra.update(|e| if let Some(slot) = e.get_mut(i) { *slot = v });
                                        }
                                    >
                                        <option value="">"Choose a filament"</option>
                                        {move || filaments.get().into_iter().map(|o| view! {
                                            <option value=o.name.clone()>{o.name.clone()}</option>
                                        }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                                    </select>
                                </label>
                            }
                        }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                        <Show when=move || extra.with(Vec::len) < MAX_EXTRA>
                            <button class="sl-btn sl-add-filament" on:click=move |_| extra.update(|e| e.push(String::new()))>
                                "Add filament"
                            </button>
                        </Show>
                    </div>
                </Show>

                <div class="sl-actions">
                    <button class="sl-btn sl-primary sl-slice" disabled=move || !can_slice() on:click=slice>
                        {move || if comparing.get() { "Slice and compare" } else { "Slice" }}
                    </button>
                    <button class="sl-btn sl-compare-toggle" class:active=move || comparing.get() on:click=toggle_compare>
                        "Compare"
                    </button>
                </div>
                {move || error.get().map(|e| view! { <p class="sl-error sl-input-error">{e}</p> })}
            </section>

            <Show when=move || !compare_ids.with(Vec::is_empty)>
                <CompareTable ids=compare_ids.into() />
            </Show>

            {move || selected.get().map(|id| view! { <JobCard id=id /> })}

            <section class="sl-recent">
                <h3 class="nd-label">"Recent"</h3>
                <ul>
                    <For each=recent_ids key=|id| *id let:id>
                        <RecentRow id=id selected=selected />
                    </For>
                </ul>
            </section>
        </div>
    }
}

#[component]
fn RecentRow(id: u64, selected: RwSignal<Option<u64>>) -> impl IntoView {
    let job = expect_context::<SlicerShared>().job(id);
    view! {
        <li
            class="sl-recent-row"
            class:active=move || selected.get() == Some(id)
            on:click=move |_| selected.set(Some(id))
        >
            <span class="sl-recent-name">{move || job.with(|j| j.as_ref().map(|j| j.model_name.clone()).unwrap_or_default())}</span>
            <span class="sl-recent-filament">{move || job.with(|j| j.as_ref().map(|j| j.filament.clone()).unwrap_or_default())}</span>
            <span class="nd-mono sl-recent-state">{move || job.with(|j| j.as_ref().map(status_line).unwrap_or_default())}</span>
        </li>
    }
}

#[component]
fn JobCard(id: u64) -> impl IntoView {
    let job = expect_context::<SlicerShared>().job(id);
    let cancel = move |_| {
        spawn_local(async move {
            let _ = bridge::cancel(id).await;
        });
    };
    let running = move || job.with(|j| j.as_ref().is_some_and(|j| !j.state.is_terminal()));
    let percent = move || {
        job.with(|j| match j.as_ref().map(|j| &j.state) {
            Some(JobState::Running { progress: Some(p) }) => p.percent,
            _ => 0,
        })
    };
    view! {
        <section class="sl-job">
            <header class="sl-job-head">
                <div>
                    <p class="sl-job-model">{move || job.with(|j| j.as_ref().map(|j| j.model_name.clone()).unwrap_or_default())}</p>
                    <p class="nd-label">
                        {move || job.with(|j| j.as_ref().map(|j| format!("{} · {} · {}", j.printer, j.process, j.filament)).unwrap_or_default())}
                    </p>
                </div>
                <Show when=running>
                    <button class="sl-btn sl-cancel" on:click=cancel>"Cancel"</button>
                </Show>
            </header>
            <p class="nd-mono sl-job-status">{move || job.with(|j| j.as_ref().map(status_line).unwrap_or_else(|| "This job is no longer available.".into()))}</p>
            <Show when=running>
                <div class="sl-progress"><div class="sl-progress-bar" style:width=move || format!("{}%", percent())></div></div>
            </Show>
            {move || job.with(|j| match j.as_ref().map(|j| &j.state) {
                Some(JobState::Failed { error }) => Some(view! { <p class="sl-error sl-job-error">{error.message.clone()}</p> }.into_any()),
                Some(JobState::Done { .. }) => Some(view! { <ResultView id=id /> }.into_any()),
                _ => None,
            })}
        </section>
    }
}

#[component]
fn ResultView(id: u64) -> impl IntoView {
    let job = expect_context::<SlicerShared>().job(id);
    let result = Memo::new(move |_| {
        job.with(|j| match j.as_ref().map(|j| &j.state) {
            Some(JobState::Done { result, .. }) => Some(result.clone()),
            _ => None,
        })
    });
    let plate = RwSignal::new(1u32);
    let thumb = RwSignal::new(None::<String>);
    Effect::new(move |_| {
        let p = plate.get();
        let has_thumb = result.with(|r| {
            r.as_ref().is_some_and(|r| {
                r.plates
                    .iter()
                    .any(|x| x.index == p && x.thumbnail.is_some())
            })
        });
        thumb.set(None);
        if !has_thumb {
            return;
        }
        spawn_local(async move {
            if let Ok(Some(url)) = bridge::thumbnail(id, p).await {
                thumb.try_set(Some(url));
            }
        });
    });
    let current = move || {
        result.with(|r| {
            r.as_ref()
                .and_then(|r| r.plates.iter().find(|x| x.index == plate.get()).cloned())
        })
    };
    let open = move |_| {
        spawn_local(async move {
            let _ = bridge::open_in_bambu_studio(id).await;
        });
    };
    view! {
        <div class="sl-result">
            <Show when=move || result.with(|r| r.as_ref().is_some_and(|r| r.plates.len() > 1))>
                <div class="sl-plate-tabs" role="tablist">
                    {move || result.with(|r| r.as_ref().map(|r| r.plates.iter().map(|p| {
                        let idx = p.index;
                        view! {
                            <button class="sl-plate-tab" class:active=move || plate.get() == idx on:click=move |_| plate.set(idx)>
                                {format!("Plate {idx}")}
                            </button>
                        }
                    }).collect::<Vec<_>>()))}
                </div>
            </Show>
            <div class="sl-result-body">
                <div class="sl-thumb">
                    {move || thumb.get().map(|src| view! { <img src=src alt="Plate preview" /> })}
                </div>
                {move || current().map(|p| {
                    let warnings = p.warnings.clone();
                    view! {
                        <div class="sl-metrics">
                            <p class="sl-hero nd-mono">{format_duration(p.time_seconds)}</p>
                            <dl class="sl-stats">
                                <div><dt class="nd-label">"Weight"</dt><dd class="sl-weight">{format_grams(p.weight_g)}</dd></div>
                                <div><dt class="nd-label">"Cost"</dt><dd class="sl-cost">{p.cost.map(format_cost).unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into())}</dd></div>
                            </dl>
                            <ul class="sl-filaments">
                                {p.filaments.iter().map(|f| view! {
                                    <li class="sl-filament">
                                        <span class="sl-swatch" style:background=f.color.clone()></span>
                                        {format!("Slot {} · {} · {} · {:.2} m", f.slot, f.filament_type, format_grams(f.used_g), f.used_m)}
                                    </li>
                                }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                            </ul>
                            <ul class="sl-warnings">
                                {warnings.into_iter().map(|w| {
                                    let level = match w.level { WarningLevel::Warning => "warning", WarningLevel::Notice => "notice" };
                                    view! { <li class=format!("sl-warning sl-warning-{level}")><span class="nd-label">{level}</span>" "{w.message}</li> }
                                }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    }
                })}
            </div>
            <button class="sl-btn sl-open" on:click=open>"Open in Bambu Studio"</button>
        </div>
    }
}

#[component]
fn CompareTable(ids: Signal<Vec<u64>>) -> impl IntoView {
    let shared = expect_context::<SlicerShared>();
    let columns = Memo::new(move |_| {
        let ids = ids.get();
        shared.jobs.with(|js| {
            ids.iter()
                .map(|id| js.iter().find(|j| j.id == *id).cloned())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        })
    });
    let rows = move || {
        let totals_per_col: Vec<_> = columns.with(|cols| {
            cols.iter()
                .map(|c| match c.as_ref().map(|j| &j.state) {
                    Some(JobState::Done { result, .. }) => Some(totals(result)),
                    _ => None,
                })
                .collect()
        });
        compare_rows(&totals_per_col)
    };
    view! {
        <section class="sl-compare">
            <table class="sl-compare-table">
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th></th>
                        {move || columns.get().into_iter().map(|c| {
                            let (name, state) = c.map(|j| (j.filament.clone(), status_line(&j))).unwrap_or_default();
                            view! { <th><span class="sl-col-name">{name}</span><span class="nd-label sl-col-state">{state}</span></th> }
                        }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    {move || rows().into_iter().map(|row| view! {
                        <tr>
                            <th class="nd-label">{row.label}</th>
                            {row.cells.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, cell)| {
                                let best = row.best == Some(i);
                                let delta = row.deltas.get(i).cloned().flatten();
                                view! {
                                    <td class:best=best>
                                        <span class="nd-mono">{cell.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into())}</span>
                                        {delta.map(|d| view! { <span class="sl-delta">{d}</span> })}
                                    </td>
                                }
                            }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                        </tr>
                    }).collect::<Vec<_>>()}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </section>
    }
}
````

Create `style/slice.css`:

````css
/* Slice page, under the .nd scope (style/tokens.css). Flat surfaces,
   borders instead of shadows, red only for failures. */

.slice-page { display: grid; gap: var(--nd-space-lg); max-width: 1040px; }
.slice-page h2 { margin: 0; font-weight: 500; color: var(--nd-text-display); }
.slice-page section {
    border: 1px solid var(--nd-border-visible);
    background: var(--nd-surface);
    padding: var(--nd-space-md);
    display: grid;
    gap: var(--nd-space-md);
}

.sl-head { display: flex; align-items: baseline; justify-content: space-between; gap: var(--nd-space-md); }
.sl-version { margin: 0; }

.sl-btn {
    padding: 8px 14px;
    border: 1px solid var(--nd-border-visible);
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--nd-text-primary);
    font-family: var(--nd-font-mono);
    font-size: 12px;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    cursor: pointer;
}
.sl-btn:hover { border-color: var(--nd-text-secondary); }
.sl-btn:disabled { opacity: 0.4; cursor: default; }
.sl-btn.active,
.sl-primary { background: var(--nd-text-display); color: var(--nd-black); border-color: var(--nd-text-display); }

.sl-drop {
    border: 1px dashed var(--nd-border-visible);
    padding: var(--nd-space-lg);
    display: grid;
    gap: var(--nd-space-sm);
    justify-items: center;
    transition: border-color 150ms var(--nd-ease);
}
.sl-drop-over { border-color: var(--nd-interactive); }
.sl-model { margin: 0; font-family: var(--nd-font-mono); color: var(--nd-text-display); }
.sl-drop-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: var(--nd-space-sm); align-items: center; }

.sl-presets,
.sl-compare-pickers {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr));
    gap: var(--nd-space-md);
    align-items: end;
}
.sl-field { display: grid; gap: var(--nd-space-xs); }
.slice-page select {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 6px 8px;
    border: 1px solid var(--nd-border-visible);
    border-radius: 4px;
    background: var(--nd-surface);
    color: var(--nd-text-primary);
    font: inherit;
}
.sl-actions { display: flex; gap: var(--nd-space-sm); }

.sl-error { margin: 0; color: var(--nd-accent); }

.sl-job-head { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: start; gap: var(--nd-space-md); }
.sl-job-model { margin: 0 0 var(--nd-space-xs); color: var(--nd-text-display); }
.sl-job-status { margin: 0; color: var(--nd-text-secondary); }
.sl-progress { height: 4px; background: var(--nd-border); }
.sl-progress-bar { height: 100%; background: var(--nd-text-display); transition: width 200ms var(--nd-ease); }

.sl-plate-tabs { display: flex; gap: var(--nd-space-xs); }
.sl-plate-tab {
    padding: 4px 10px;
    border: 1px solid var(--nd-border-visible);
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--nd-text-secondary);
    cursor: pointer;
    font-family: var(--nd-font-mono);
    font-size: 12px;
}
.sl-plate-tab.active { color: var(--nd-text-display); border-color: var(--nd-text-display); }
.sl-result { display: grid; gap: var(--nd-space-md); }
.sl-result-body { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(160px, 240px) 1fr; gap: var(--nd-space-lg); }
.sl-thumb { aspect-ratio: 1; background: var(--nd-surface-raised); border: 1px solid var(--nd-border); }
.sl-thumb img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: contain; display: block; }
.sl-metrics { display: grid; gap: var(--nd-space-md); align-content: start; }
.sl-hero { margin: 0; font-size: 48px; line-height: 1; color: var(--nd-text-display); }
.sl-stats { display: flex; gap: var(--nd-space-xl); margin: 0; }
.sl-stats dd { margin: var(--nd-space-xs) 0 0; font-family: var(--nd-font-mono); color: var(--nd-text-display); }
.sl-filaments,
.sl-warnings { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: grid; gap: var(--nd-space-xs); }
.sl-filament { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--nd-space-sm); font-family: var(--nd-font-mono); font-size: 13px; }
.sl-swatch { width: 12px; height: 12px; border: 1px solid var(--nd-border-visible); }
.sl-warning { padding: var(--nd-space-sm); border-left: 2px solid var(--nd-border-visible); color: var(--nd-text-primary); }
.sl-warning-warning { border-left-color: var(--nd-warning); }
.sl-open { justify-self: start; }

.sl-compare-table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }
.sl-compare-table th,
.sl-compare-table td { padding: var(--nd-space-sm); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--nd-border); text-align: left; vertical-align: top; }
.sl-compare-table td.best { color: var(--nd-success); }
.sl-col-name { display: block; color: var(--nd-text-display); font-weight: 500; }
.sl-col-state { display: block; }
.sl-delta { display: block; font-family: var(--nd-font-mono); font-size: 11px; color: var(--nd-text-secondary); }

.sl-recent ul { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
.sl-recent h3 { margin: 0; }
.sl-recent-row {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr auto;
    gap: var(--nd-space-md);
    padding: var(--nd-space-sm) 0;
````

````rust
}

/// The newest job sliced from `source_path`.
pub fn latest_for_source<'a>(jobs: &'a [JobView], source_path: &str) -> Option<&'a JobView> {
    jobs.iter().rev().find(|j| j.source_path == source_path)
}

/// The STL indicator's per-file state: "Slicing…", "2h 14m · 38 g" or
/// "Slice failed". `None` for a cancelled job.
pub fn badge_text(job: &JobView) -> Option<String> {
    match &job.state {
        JobState::Queued { .. } | JobState::Running { .. } => Some("Slicing…".to_string()),
        JobState::Done { result, .. } => {
            let t = totals(result);
            Some(format!(
                "{} · {}",
                format_duration(t.time_seconds),
                format_grams(t.weight_g)
            ))
        }
        JobState::Failed { .. } => Some("Slice failed".to_string()),
        JobState::Cancelled => None,
    }
}

````

Create `src/components/slicer_settings.rs`:

````rust
//! Settings → Application: auto-slicing and the slice cache.

use leptos::prelude::*;
use wasm_bindgen_futures::spawn_local;

use crate::slicer::bridge;
use crate::slicer::types::SlicerSettings;

#[component]
pub fn SlicerSettingsSection() -> impl IntoView {
    let saved = RwSignal::new(None::<SlicerSettings>);
    let auto_status = RwSignal::new(None::<String>);
    let cache_status = RwSignal::new(None::<String>);

    spawn_local(async move {
        if let Ok(view) = bridge::get_settings().await {
            saved.try_set(Some(view.saved));
        }
    });

    let on_toggle = move |ev: leptos::ev::Event| {
        let enabled = event_target_checked(&ev);
        let Some(previous) = saved.get_untracked() else {
            return;
        };
        let next = SlicerSettings {
            auto_slice: enabled,
            ..previous.clone()
        };
        // Show the change at once; roll back if the backend refuses it.
        saved.set(Some(next.clone()));
        spawn_local(async move {
            match bridge::set_settings(next).await {
                Ok(view) => {
                    saved.try_set(Some(view.saved));
                    auto_status.try_set(Some(if enabled {
                        "New STLs will be sliced with your default printer, process and filament."
                            .to_string()
                    } else {
                        "Auto-slicing is off.".to_string()
                    }));
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    saved.try_set(Some(previous));
                    auto_status.try_set(Some(format!("Failed to save: {e}")));
                }
            }
        });
    };

    let clear = move |_| {
        spawn_local(async move {
            let text = match bridge::clear_cache().await {
                Ok(bytes) => format!("Cleared {:.1} MB.", bytes as f64 / 1_048_576.0),
                Err(e) => format!("Failed: {e}"),
            };
            cache_status.try_set(Some(text));
        });
    };

    view! {
        <div class="form-group slicer-settings">
            <label class="checkbox-label" style="display: inline-flex; gap: 0.4rem;">
                <input
                    id="slice-auto"
                    type="checkbox"
                    disabled=move || saved.with(Option::is_none)
                    prop:checked=move || saved.with(|s| s.as_ref().is_some_and(|s| s.auto_slice))
                    on:change=on_toggle
                />
                "Slice new STLs automatically"
            </label>
            <p class="section-description">
                "Uses the printer, process and filament you last sliced with on the Slice page."
            </p>
            <Show when=move || auto_status.get().is_some()>
                <span class="status-text slice-auto-status">{move || auto_status.get().unwrap_or_default()}</span>
            </Show>
            <div class="input-row">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm slice-clear-cache" on:click=clear>
                    "Clear slice cache"
                </button>
            </div>
            <Show when=move || cache_status.get().is_some()>
                <span class="status-text slice-cache-status">{move || cache_status.get().unwrap_or_default()}</span>
            </Show>
        </div>
    }
}
````

````rust
use leptos::prelude::*;
use wasm_bindgen::JsCast;
use wasm_bindgen_futures::spawn_local;

use crate::commands::{self, StlFile};

#[component]
pub fn StlIndicator() -> impl IntoView {
    let (stl_files, set_stl_files) = signal::<Vec<StlFile>>(vec![]);
    let (expanded, set_expanded) = signal(false);

    // Poll for STL files every 5 seconds
    Effect::new(move |_| {
        let callback = wasm_bindgen::closure::Closure::wrap(Box::new(move || {
            spawn_local(async move {
                if let Ok(files) = commands::list_received_stls().await {
                    set_stl_files.set(files);
                }
            });
        }) as Box<dyn Fn()>);
````

````rust
            spawn_local(async move {
                if let Ok(files) = commands::list_received_stls().await {
````

````rust
                                            <button
                                                class="btn-small"
                                                on:click=move |_| open_in_bs(path_open.clone())
                                            >
                                                "Open in BS"
                                            </button>
                                            <button
                                                class="btn-small btn-dismiss"
                                                on:click=move |_| dismiss(path_dismiss.clone())
                                            >
                                                "Dismiss"
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                }
                            }
                        />
                    </div>
                </Show>
            </div>
````

In `style/slice.css`, replace:

````css
@media (max-width: 720px) {
    .sl-result-body { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
}
````

with:

````css
@media (max-width: 720px) {
    .sl-result-body { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
}

/* Auto-slice state in the sidebar's STL list (outside the .nd scope). */
.stl-slice-state {
    margin: 2px 0;
    padding: 0;
    border: none;
    background: none;
    color: inherit;
    font: inherit;
    font-size: 11px;
    opacity: 0.8;
    cursor: pointer;
    text-align: left;
}
.stl-slice-state:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
````

Run: `cargo test --bin bambumate`
Expected: `test result: ok. 17 passed; 0 failed`.

Run: `trunk build && cd tests/webkit && node app-flows.mjs ../..`
Expected: `webkit: 55/55 steps passed`, `chromium: 55/55 steps passed`, `PASS: every flow completed in both engines.`

Run: `cd tests/webkit && node css-compat.mjs ../.. && node layout.mjs ../..`
Expected: `PASS: no WebKit-only CSS problems.` and `PASS: all full-screen overlays cover the viewport in both engines.`

Then run every command in Global Constraints → Verification commands. Expected: all pass (`cd src-tauri && cargo test` has 3 ignored tests that need a local Bambu Studio).

Run: `cargo fmt --check`
Expected: no output.

Run: `cargo check --target wasm32-unknown-unknown 2>&1 | grep -E "src/(slicer|pages/slice|components/(stl_indicator|slicer_settings))"`

## Manual acceptance (on the user's Mac)

1. `cd src-tauri && cargo test -- --ignored`: the three ignored tests pass against the installed Bambu Studio 02.08.02.61.
2. `cargo tauri dev`, open **Slice**: the header reads `BAMBU STUDIO 02.08.02.61` and the printer is the H2C. Choose a model, press **Slice**, watch the progress line, then press **Open in Bambu Studio**. Bambu Studio opens the sliced file; nothing is sent to the printer.
3. Slice the same model again: it shows `Done · from cache` at once.
4. Press **Compare**, pick a PETG, press **Slice and compare**: two columns, the best value in each row in green, deltas against the first column.
5. In Settings, turn on **Slice new STLs automatically** and save an STL into the watch folder: the STL list shows `Slicing…`, then a `14m · 3.69 g`-style line; clicking it opens the job on `/slice`.
6. Ask the agent to "slice ~/Downloads/cube.stl and compare PLA with PETG": it calls `bm_slice` and reports time, weight and cost without printing.
